We evaluate our approach on a newly constructed dataset for the task of generating replies on social media. Experimental results show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art ICL-based baselines, while our evaluation protocol achieves moderate correlation with human judgment. Besides, experiments on fictional-character benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed method is applicable beyond the social media setting. These findings suggest that incorporating behavioral information broadly improves the fidelity of role-playing for real individuals on social media or fictional characters.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Atomic Tokens: Factorizing Syllables for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21362)
*Nghia Hieu Nguyen,Thai Bao Huynh,Binh-An Dinh-Le,Phu Gia Hoang,Dat Tien Nguyen,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出音位分词器，将越南语和汉语音节转为IPA并分解为声母、韵母、声调三要素，共享一个上下文位置，词表极小（中文112、越南语256），并以此构建PhonemicBERT取得竞争性效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分词器以字符或统计子词表示文本，忽视音节的内部音系结构，且往往需要大词表。作者希望以语言学驱动的音位分解方式，实现紧凑、高效且可解释的文本表示。

Method: 将每个音节转写为IPA，并分解为声母、韵母、声调三个音系成分；三者共同占据一个上下文位置，在保持音节级序列长度的同时实现音系相关音节间的表示共享；非音系及不支持单元采用字符级回退。该确定性设计无需依赖语料的词表学习。进一步构建PhonemicBERT，结合分解式成分嵌入，并用三个预测头重建被掩码的完整音节。

Result: 内在评测显示两种语言的Rényi效率显著更高；对标准越南语音节词典中每个条目Fertility恰为1，且越南语序列通常比现有预训练分词器更短。在受控中文预训练设置下，PhonemicBERT-Zh在多项语言理解任务上与字符、子词及SubChar方案相当或更优；PhonemicBERT-Vi也取得与既有越南语及多语种预训练模型相当或更优的结果。

Conclusion: 音位分解是一种紧凑、高效且可解释的文本表示方案，可作为原子化表示与统计分词的替代选择。

Abstract: Conventional tokenizers represent text as characters or statistically derived subwords, overlooking the internal phonological structure of syllables and often requiring large vocabularies. We introduce \textbf{Phonemic Tokenizer}, a linguistically motivated tokenizer for Vietnamese and Chinese that converts each syllable into IPA and factorizes it into three phonological components: onset, rime, and tone. The three components jointly occupy one contextual position, preserving syllable-level sequence length while enabling representation sharing across phonologically related syllables. Non-phonological and unsupported units are handled through character-level fallback. This deterministic design requires no corpus-dependent vocabulary learning and yields vocabularies of only 112 entries for Chinese and 256 for Vietnamese. Intrinsic evaluation shows that the tokenizer achieves substantially higher Rényi efficiency in both languages, represents every entry in a standard Vietnamese syllable dictionary with a Fertility of exactly one, and generally produces shorter Vietnamese sequences than existing pretrained tokenizers. We further instantiate the tokenizer in \textbf{PhonemicBERT}, which combines factorized component embeddings and reconstructs complete masked syllables using three prediction heads. Under a controlled Chinese pretraining setup, PhonemicBERT-Zh is competitive with or outperforms character, subword, and SubChar alternatives across diverse language-understanding tasks. PhonemicBERT-Vi also achieves competitive or superior results to established Vietnamese and multilingual pretrained models. These results establish phonemic factorization as a compact, efficient, and interpretable alternative to atomic and statistically segmented text representations.

</details>


### [39] [ArenaFlow: From Trajectory Ranking to Hierarchical Credit Propagation for Open-Ended Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21378)
*Qiang Zhang,Ruixue Ding,Fanrui Zhang,Xi Chen,Boli Chen,Shihang Wang,Yinfeng Huang,Yi Zheng,Pengjun Xie,Kaipeng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出分层信用传播框架 ArenaFlow，用于开放式智能体强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在可验证领域显著提升大语言模型智能体，但在开放式任务中难以应用：解多样、可靠的标量奖励难获得。成对评估虽缓解奖励判别坍缩，却将丰富比较反馈压缩为单一轨迹级奖励，掩盖关键中间步骤，且无法把成功行为固化为可复用技能。

Method: ArenaFlow 利用锦标赛式相对排名得到轨迹级奖励信号；每次比较附带结构化反思评估，挖掘三类监督：关键成功步骤、可复用策略技能、检索技能的使用归因。步骤级按锦标赛存活深度将轨迹级优势传播到高置信关键步骤；技能级由组级使用归因估计技能效用，并通过效用感知的更新、剪枝与检索维护全局技能记忆，高效用技能作为未来探索的策略先验。

Result: 大量实验验证了 ArenaFlow 在开放式智能体任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: ArenaFlow 通过分层信用传播，将轨迹级偏好奖励细化为步骤级与技能级监督，缓解开放式智能体强化学习中的信用分配与技能复用问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has substantially improved large language model (LLM) agents in verifiable domains, but remains difficult to apply to open-ended agent tasks, where solutions are diverse and reliable scalar rewards are hard to obtain. Recent pairwise evaluation methods alleviate reward discrimination collapse by replacing pointwise scoring with relative preferences. However, they still compress rich comparative feedback into a single trajectory-level reward, obscuring decisive intermediate steps and preventing successful behaviors from being consolidated into reusable skills. We propose ArenaFlow, a hierarchical credit propagation framework for open-ended agent reinforcement learning. ArenaFlow leverages tournament-based relative ranking to derive trajectory-level reward signals. Each comparison is further equipped with structured reflective evaluation, which reveals three types of supervision: pivotal success steps, reusable strategy skills, and usage attribution of retrieved skills. At the step level, ArenaFlow propagates trajectory-level advantages to high-confidence pivotal steps according to tournament survival depth, enabling more targeted optimization of local reasoning behaviors. At the skill level, ArenaFlow estimates skill utility from group-level usage attribution and maintains a global skill memory through utility-aware updating, pruning, and retrieval. The resulting high-utility skills further serve as policy priors for future exploration. Extensive experiments validate ArenaFlow's effectiveness on open-ended agent tasks.

</details>


### [40] [Prediction Dynamics in Depth-Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21383)
*Xinyue Luo,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文通过边际刻画分解深度循环语言模型得分变化的来源，解释了中间答案与最终答案一致但得分持续变化的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度循环语言模型通过重复的潜在更新精炼预测：中间答案可与终点一致，但其得分仍持续变化，这一现象缺乏精确解释。作者希望刻画得分的保守性来源，理解有限深度下答案保持的机制。

Method: 推导精确的边际刻画，将幅度界的保守性分解为三部分：共同平移、相对胜者的方向、以及每个竞争者更新与其得分差的配对。同时将共同运动与对比运动正交分离，通过候选集质量和集合内集中度表达共同分量。在Huginn-3.5B与Ouro-1.4B上，基于完整轨迹进行回溯性比较，并在完整答案文本评分与标签评分两种设置下评估。

Result: 在完整答案文本评分下，引入更新方向与竞争者配对后，平均最早合格深度相比仅去除平移进一步降低总深度的22.5–34.4%；在标签评分下也有显著贡献。共同与对比能量可以以不同速率衰减，使得偏好变化占比增长与绝对更新缩小并存。

Conclusion: 得分变化的几何与组成结构可解释有限深度下的答案保持：平移、方向与配对分别贡献保守性，共同与对比运动的差异化衰减使相对偏好变化与绝对更新解耦。

Abstract: Depth-recurrent language models refine predictions through repeated latent updates. Why can intermediate answers agree with the endpoint while their scores continue to change? We derive a sharp margin characterization that decomposes the conservatism of a magnitude bound into common translation, direction relative to the winner, and the pairing of each competitor's update with its score gap. Across Huginn-3.5B and Ouro-1.4B, accounting for update direction and competitor pairing reduces the mean earliest qualifying depth by a further 22.5-34.4% of the total depth beyond translation removal under full answer-text scoring. This retrospective comparison uses completed trajectories. Substantial contributions also occur under label scoring. For shared predictive distributions, we separate common and contrast motion orthogonally and express the common component through candidate-set mass and within-set concentration. Common and contrast energies can attenuate at different rates, allowing a growing preference-change share to coexist with shrinking absolute updates. These findings explain finite-depth answer preservation through the geometry and composition of observed score changes.

</details>


### [41] [Consistent Relexicalization of Clinical Documents using Graph-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21387)
*Dipankar Das,Atri Mandal,Sandeep Singh,Tushar Shandhilya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: G-RELIC 结合图结构与LLM，通过一对一的图映射机制和确定性时序重定位算法实现临床文本再词汇化，在保持隐私基准的同时显著提升关系完整性与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 再词汇化在临床NLP中至关重要，可对敏感信息进行掩码并合成保留真实特征的数据集。但现有方法多依赖独立实体替换，导致纵向病历中的临床不一致性，削弱了再词汇化数据集对下游科学分析的价值。因此需要同时保证结构完整性、关系连贯性与时序一致性。

Method: 提出 G-RELIC（Graph Based Contextual Relexicalization with Improved Consistency），将大语言模型与图结构相结合。核心包括：基于图的映射机制，优化原始实体与替代实体之间的一对一对应关系；以及确定性的时序重定位算法，用于保持时序一致性。

Result: 在多个真实临床数据集上的实证评估表明，G-RELIC 显著优于当前最先进的基线方法：关系完整性从 62.1% 提升至 92.5%，提高 30.4 个百分点；时序一致性从 46% 提升至 91.9%，提高 45.9 个百分点；同时未牺牲临床数据集公认的隐私基准。

Conclusion: G-RELIC 在保证隐私保护水平的前提下，最大化了再词汇化数据集的分析效用，同时最小化了重识别风险，为临床NLP中的一致性再词汇化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Relexicalization is a pivotal technique in clinical NLP, as it facilitates robust masking of sensitive information while synthesizing datasets that retain high-fidelity, real-world characteristics. However, preserving structural integrity, relational coherence, and temporal consistency during transformation remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches frequently rely on independent entity replacement, which results in clinical inconsistencies across longitudinal records. This reduces the value of such relexicalized datasets for downstream scientific analysis. To address these limitations, we introduce G-RELIC (Graph Based Contextual Relexicalization with Improved Consistency) which combines the power of LLMs with graphs. G-RELIC implements a graph-based mapping mechanism which optimizes for one-to-one correspondence between original and surrogate entities. It also introduces a deterministic temporal repositioning algorithm to preserve temporal consistency. Empirical evaluations on diverse, real-world clinical datasets validate that G-RELIC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. G-RELIC yields a 30.4 percentage point improvement in relational integrity (62.1% to 92.5%) and 45.9 percentage point improvement in temporal coherence (46% to 91.9%) without compromising on the recognized privacy benchmarks for clinical datasets. This maximizes the analytical utility of relexicalized datasets while minimizing re-identification risk.

</details>


### [42] [Omni Demand Understanding: A Benchmark for Contextual User-Intent Inference in Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21392)
*Qi Chen,Yunfei Chu,Haolin He,Yifan Yang,Zihan Liu,Yuxuan Wang,Ziyang Ma,Ruiyang Xu,Meng Gao,Yinsong Yan,Ling Wang,Hui Wang,Wen Huang,Yiheng Chen,Guanrou Yang,Qiuqiang Kong,Jin Xu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ODU（Omni Demand Understanding）这一多模态语境推断问题，并构建ODU-Bench基准，评测MLLM能否从复杂多模态交互中正确推断用户潜在需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互能力基准主要关注响应质量，忽视了更根本的问题：模型能否从复杂多模态交互中正确推断用户底层需求。真实需求常在语音中表述不充分，需结合多模态线索与对话历史推断，且受模糊/不流畅表达、噪声环境影响；同时请求式语音未必构成真实需求，易导致误触发。

Method: 将ODU定义为独立的多模态语境推断问题：给定交互流，模型需判断是否存在用户需求，并从多模态与对话语境中推断意图。评估涵盖五个维度、包含单轮与多轮交互。通过挑战驱动分类法、分类法引导的智能体视频生成及真人录制交互构建ODU-Bench，并进行基于媒体的标注与人工验证。评测14个原生MLLM。

Result: 最强模型Gemini 3.1 Pro仅能恢复44.7%必须从视觉、声学或对话语境推断的关键信息；14个模型中有11个在非需求场景的误触发率超过50%。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了当前MLLM在推断语境化用户需求方面存在系统性能力差距。作者希望ODU能确立对多模态交互中此前未被充分探索但至关重要的能力评估：在生成合适响应之前正确理解用户需求。

Abstract: Natural audio-visual interaction is emerging as an important interface for AI assistants, allowing users to communicate through speech and vision rather than carefully composed text prompts. However, existing benchmarks of interactive capabilities still focus primarily on response quality, leaving a more fundamental question underexplored: can a model correctly infer the user's underlying demand from complex multimodal interaction? Real-world user demands are often underspecified in speech and must be inferred from multimodal cues and dialogue history. This inference is further complicated by ambiguous or disfluent expression and noisy acoustic environments. Conversely, request-like speech may not constitute a demand to the assistant, leading to false triggers. We establish Omni Demand Understanding (ODU) as a distinct multimodal contextual inference problem: given an interaction stream, a model must detect whether a user demand is present and infer intent from multimodal and conversational context. ODU evaluates this capability along five dimensions, covering both single-turn and multi-turn interactions. We construct ODU-Bench using a challenge-driven taxonomy, taxonomy-guided agentic video generation, and human-recorded interactions, followed by media-grounded annotation and human verification. We evaluate 14 native MLLMs. Even the strongest, Gemini 3.1 Pro, recovers only 44.7% of key information that must be inferred from visual, acoustic, or conversational context. Moreover, 11 of the 14 models exhibit false-trigger rates above 50% on non-demand scenarios. These results reveal a systematic capability gap in current MLLMs' ability to infer contextual user demands. We hope ODU can establish the evaluation of a previously underexplored yet essential capability in multimodal interaction: correctly understanding user demands before generating an appropriate response.

</details>


### [43] [Talking Past the Machine: Morality, Politeness, and Alignment in Human-AI Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21401)
*Marina Mitiaeva,Lu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过分析15,881段人-ChatGPT与10,784段人人多轮对话，发现AI只复制了合作沟通的表面特征，而缺乏支撑人际适应的社会结构：道德为预设而非协商、温暖输出缺乏面子敏感性、语言趋同持续下降，且部分合作机制（如模糊限制、软化、纯洁框架）在人机互动中作用方向反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI能生成流利、社交得体的回应，但其究竟参与了合作沟通，还是仅模拟了合作沟通的表面形式，尚不清楚；这一问题直接关系到此类系统如何被评估、信任与设计。研究旨在比较人机互动与人际对话中合作对话的三个核心维度——道德、礼貌与对齐——的运作差异。

Method: 分析15,881段人-ChatGPT对话与10,784段人人多轮对话，采用混合效应模型（mixed-effects models）识别能够预测逐轮对齐（turn-to-turn alignment）的特征。

Result: 观察到一致的分离现象：AI产出合作沟通的表面特征，却缺乏其背后的社会架构。道德输出看似预先配置而非协商生成；温暖被生成却不具备面子敏感性；语言趋同持续下降。最显著的是合作机制本身方向反转：在人際之间与更高顺应性相关的模糊限制与软化，由AI产出时却与更低对齐相关；与人类分歧相关的纯洁框架，在AI情景下却伴随用户向AI趋同。能动性（为用户留出塑造交流的空间）是两类互动中一致最强的对齐预测因子；而较新模型中道德断言性降低并未伴随合作改善。

Conclusion: 这些模式表明，AI复制了合作的表面，却缺少支撑人际合作的相互适应；更意外的是，维系人类顺应的机制在人机互动中可能反向运行。这提示仅从逐轮层面考察，可能不足以判断互动层面的成功。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems produce fluent, socially appropriate responses, yet whether they participate in cooperative communication or merely simulate its surface forms remains unclear - a question central to how these systems are evaluated, trusted, and designed. This study investigates how morality, politeness, and alignment - three dimensions central to cooperative dialogue - function in human-AI interaction compared to human-human conversation. We analyze 15,881 human-ChatGPT and 10,784 human-human multi-turn dialogues, using mixed-effects models to identify which features predict turn-to-turn alignment. We observe a consistent dissociation: AI produces the surface features of cooperative communication without the underlying social architecture. Moral output appears preconfigured rather than negotiated; warmth is generated without face sensitivity; linguistic convergence declines persistently. Most strikingly, the cooperative mechanisms themselves reverse direction: hedging and softening associated with greater accommodation between humans are associated with reduced alignment when produced by AI, and purity framing associated with human divergence coincides with users converging toward the AI. Agency - giving users room to shape the exchange - is the most consistent predictor of alignment across both interaction types, while lower moral assertiveness in more recent models is not accompanied by better cooperation. Together these patterns suggest that AI reproduces the surface of cooperation without the mutual adaptation that grounds it between humans - and, more surprisingly, that mechanisms sustaining human accommodation can run in reverse with AI, suggesting a turn-level view may be insufficient for interaction-level success.

</details>


### [44] [Benchmarking Gender Bias in Machine Translation Evaluation Metrics across Occupations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21490)
*Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Giorgos Filandrianos,Wafaa Mohammed,Giuseppe Attanasio,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究基于GAMBIT+构建职业均衡子集，考察WMT 2026自动翻译质量评估共享任务中的性别偏见，发现男性化译文总体得分更高，且偏好程度因评估者和语言而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译在源文本未指定人物性别时，译文可能采用阳性或阴性形式，而翻译系统与自动评估指标可能对这种源文无依据的区分表现出系统性偏好，这一性别偏见问题在生成与评估两端均持续存在，需要深入考察。

Method: 使用GAMBIT+的职业均衡子集，涵盖七个英语源语言对（阿拉伯语、捷克语、希腊语、冰岛语、俄语、乌克兰语及新增的德语）。每个目标语言含1,308对阳性/阴性译文，覆盖436个ISCO-08职业组，每组3个示例。对共享任务提交系统与基线在得分预测和错误标注两方面进行评估，分析性别相关差异的方向、幅度与频率。

Result: 总体上男性化译文倾向于获得更高分数，且不同职业间存在符合刻板性别印象的差异；但这种偏好的强度与一致性在评估者和语言之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 性别偏见在机器翻译评估中依然存在，但刻画其程度不能仅依赖单一聚合指标，而需结合评估者行为的多个互补维度。

Abstract: Gender bias remains a persistent concern in machine translation (MT), affecting both generated translations and their automatic evaluation. When a source text leaves a person's gender unspecified, translations may realize that person using masculine or feminine forms, and both MT systems and evaluation metrics may exhibit systematic preferences between these alternatives despite the source providing no basis for such a distinction. We study this behavior in the WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Systems Shared Task using an occupation-balanced subset of GAMBIT+. We consider seven English-source language pairs, six from the original dataset, targeting Arabic, Czech, Greek, Icelandic, Russian, and Ukrainian, and extend the original resource with German. The subset contains 1,308 masculine/feminine translation pairs per target language, with three examples for each of the 436 ISCO-08 occupational groups. We evaluate shared-task submissions and baselines for score prediction and error annotation, examining the direction, magnitude, and frequency of gender-related differences. We find an overall tendency for masculine translations to receive higher scores, as well as differences per occupation following stereotypical gender representations, although the strength and consistency of this preference vary considerably across evaluators and languages. Our results show that gender bias remains present in MT evaluation, but that capturing its extent requires looking beyond a single aggregate measure to complementary dimensions of evaluator behavior.

</details>


### [45] [MIRAGE: Multi-Perspective Creative Language Model Reasoning with Reinforcement Learning Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21554)
*Arash Lagzian,Srinivas Anumasa,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIRAGE：一种受人类认知灵活性启发的推理时创造性思维框架，通过Selector选择有效概念视角、Reasoner顺序求解并在不确定时聚合多视角，在多个推理基准上超越CoT等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽取得显著进展，但在复杂数学、科学和逻辑任务上仍表现不佳。受人类认知灵活性（动态切换思维视角的能力）启发，作者希望提升LLM在推理时的解题能力。

Method: 提出MIRAGE（多视角推理时推理与智能体引导探索）框架，包含两个组件：Selector负责优先选择有效的概念视角（如代数、概率）；Reasoner顺序求解任务，直到得到高置信度解，否则聚合多个视角的结果。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH500、MMLU-Pro和Game-of-24基准上测试，MIRAGE持续优于Chain-of-Thought及多种提示集成方法，在极小的推理开销下显著提升准确率。

Conclusion: MIRAGE为实际应用提供了一种可扩展的推理增强解决方案，验证了多视角认知启发式在提升LLM复杂推理能力上的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized artificial intelligence and how human interact with AIs. Despite impressive advancements, LLMs struggle with complex mathematical, scientific, and logical tasks. Inspired by human cognitive flexibility - our ability to dynamically switch mental perspectives - we propose MIRAGE (Multi-perspective Inference-time Reasoning via Agent-Guided Exploration), a novel inference-time creative thinking framework. MIRAGE includes a Selector that prioritizes effective conceptual perspectives (e.g., algebraic, probabilistic) and a Reasoner that sequentially solves tasks until a confident solution emerges, otherwise aggregating multiple perspectives. Tested on GSM8K, MATH500, MMLU-Pro, and Game-of-24 benchmarks, MIRAGE consistently outperforms methods like Chain-of-Thought and diverse prompting ensembles, significantly boosting accuracy with minimal inference overhead, providing a scalable solution for practical applications.

</details>


### [46] [Evaluating In-Context Learning and Retrieval Strategies for Devanagari Post-OCR Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21595)
*Abhishek Bhandari,Gaurav Harit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统评估LLM对天城文（印地语、马拉地语）OCR后纠错，提出CharBM25检索策略，无需训练与GPU即可显著降低WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的上下文学习为免训练的OCR后纠错提供了可行路径，但其在天城文字上的有效性尚未被探索，因此需要系统评估。

Method: 在印地语和马拉地语上评测3B–32B参数规模的LLM，比较三种上下文示例检索策略：领域随机选择、稠密语义检索与提出的CharBM25（基于OCR输入的字符n-gram BM25相似度检索示例），在覆盖五个新闻领域的2万句基准上测试。

Result: 检索策略是纠错质量的决定性因素：CharBM25较领域随机选择在印地语上绝对WER改善2.8–4.0pp、马拉地语2.9–3.8pp，字符三元组优于二元与一元；Gemma-3-27B在CharBM25-5下印地语WER降低55.0%、马拉地语33.3%；8B以下模型难以稳定优于OCR基线，3B模型在马拉地语上使更多句子变差；马拉地语纠错难度始终高于印地语，反映其形态复杂度更高。

Conclusion: CharBM25是一种高效、无需GPU的检索策略，在极低计算成本下达到或超过稠密检索；与12B以上通用LLM结合可实现可靠、免微调的天城文OCR后纠错。

Abstract: In-context learning using Large Language Models (LLMs) offers a compelling path to training-free post-OCR correction, yet its effectiveness for Devanagari script remains entirely unexplored. We present the first systematic evaluation of LLMs (3B-32B) for post-OCR correction in Hindi and Marathi, comparing three in-context example retrieval strategies: domain-random selection, dense semantic retrieval, and our proposed CharBM25, which retrieves examples by character n-gram BM25 similarity over OCR inputs to target shared error patterns with the test sentence. Across a 20,000-sentence benchmark spanning five news domains, retrieval strategy is the decisive factor in correction quality: CharBM25 outperforms domain-random selection by 2.8-4.0pp absolute WER on Hindi and 2.9-3.8pp on Marathi, using character trigrams, which consistently outperform bigrams and unigrams. Scale dominates performance: Gemma-3-27B achieves WER reductions of 55.0% for Hindi and 33.3% for Marathi under CharBM25-5. Few-shot gains are capacity-gated: models below 8B do not reliably improve over the OCR baseline, and on Marathi the smallest models (3B) degrade more sentences than they improve. Marathi is persistently harder to correct than Hindi across all scales, reflecting its greater morphological complexity. These findings establish CharBM25 as an effective, GPU-free retrieval strategy that matches or exceeds dense retrieval at negligible computational cost, and show that combining it with a general-purpose LLM of 12B+ parameters delivers reliable, training-free Devanagari post-OCR correction without task-specific fine-tuning. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/AbhishekBhandari/Devanagari-OCR-ICL-Benchmark

</details>


### [47] [Steering LLMs Responses Towards Moral Foundations on the Norwegian MFQ-30](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21636)
*Hans Andersen,David Dichas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将挪威道德基础问卷(MFQ-30)施测于6个开源LLM，并与N=1282挪威人类样本对比，测试提示级人格引导与激活级ActAdd两种干预。结果显示仅半数模型真正作答，中性北欧受访者人格可将这些模型在Mahalanobis d²上向挪威均值拉近44–77%，而ActAdd只会压平道德基础剖面而非定向引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究常用人类心理测量问卷为LLM刻画道德与价值画像，但尚不清楚这些工具是否测量到模型中的稳定特质，也不明确所得画像能否被引导至特定人类目标人群。为检验测量的稳定性与可引导性，作者开展了本项工作。

Method: 向6个开源权重LLM施测挪威版道德基础问卷(MFQ-30)，将其道德基础剖面与N=1282的挪威受访者样本进行比较；采用两种引导干预：提示级人格引导(persona steering)与激活级ActAdd(固定中层单对引导向量)，并以注意力检查区分模型是否真正作答，用Mahalanobis d²度量与人类均值的距离。

Result: 半数模型通过注意力检查并真正处理题目内容；另一半模型退化为平坦或中心趋势输出，在平均值上看似接近人类却不随题目内容变化。一个不包含任何人类样本分布信息的中性北欧受访者人格，可使前一类模型在Mahalanobis d²上向挪威均值靠近44–77%。固定中层的单对ActAdd引导使道德基础剖面整体趋于平坦，而非按维度定向调整。至少有一个模型中，能改变剖面的人格同时诱发了基线时并不存在的答题参与，构成Peereboom等(2025)所警示的"认知幻影"的具体实例。

Conclusion: LLM在心理测量问卷上的表现具有异质性：部分模型并不真正作答其内容，因而其"近人类"的平坦剖面属于中心趋势假象而非稳定的价值特质。提示级人格引导可在无需分布信息的情况下有效向目标人群靠拢，而激活级ActAdd缺乏定向性。人格引导可能同时改变测量结果与作答参与度，这提示基于问卷的LLM价值画像须谨慎解释，并警惕"认知幻影"式的假性对应。

Abstract: Recent work applies human psychometric questionnaires to large language models to elicit moral and value profiles, but it is not clear whether these instruments measure anything stable in models or whether the resulting profiles can be moved toward a target human population. We administer the Norwegian Moral Foundations Questionnaire (MFQ-30) to six open-weight LLMs and compare their foundation profiles to a sample of N = 1,282 Norwegian respondents. We test two steering interventions, prompt-level persona steering and activation-level ActAdd. Half the models engage with the questionnaire under our attention check. The other half default to flat or central-tendency outputs that look near-human on average without tracking item content. A neutral Nordic-respondent persona, written without any distributional information from the human sample, brings the engaging models 44-77% closer to the Norwegian mean in Mahalanobis $d^2$. One-pair ActAdd at a fixed mid-layer flattens the foundation profile rather than steering individual foundations. For at least one model the same persona that shifts the profile also induces engagement that was absent at baseline, a concrete instance of the cognitive phantoms that Peereboom et al. (2025) warn about.

</details>


### [48] [Chinese Competitive Debating Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21637)
*Zongrui Yang,Haoyuan Li,Zhongsheng Wang,Zhirui Zeng,Pengqian Han,Yi Zhou,Yuting Wang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个结合真实竞技中文辩论完整转录与专业裁判评分的多层级数据集与基准，用于评估大语言模型对互动式论证的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论裁决需要追踪论证在互动中的发展，但现有数据集很少能同时提供细粒度辩论转录与真实比赛中的专业评判结果，导致缺乏统一的评测基准。

Method: 组织182场比赛并招募120名专业裁判，每场由三名裁判依据预设评分标准独立裁决；剔除记录不完整的比赛后保留148场、2,698个环节、20,542个交锋单元，转录与切分均经人工核验，并保留原始环节得分、比赛投票、最佳辩手选票及裁决理由。据此定义胜方倾向预测、环节得分预测和最佳辩手预测三项任务。

Result: 多模型零样本评测显示，胜方预测最高准确率66.2%，模型环节得分与人类平均评分的最高皮尔逊相关系数为0.250，最佳辩手预测最高准确率56.8%。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准为研究大语言模型对互动式论证的理解及其与专业裁判的一致性提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Debate adjudication requires tracking how arguments develop through interaction, yet existing datasets rarely combine fine-grained debate transcripts with professional judgments collected during real competitions under a shared rubric. We introduce a dataset and benchmark for evaluating large language models' understanding of competitive Chinese-language debate at the match, stage, and speaker levels. We organized 182 matches and recruited 120 professional judges, with each match independently adjudicated by three judges using a predefined rubric. After excluding matches with incomplete records, the dataset contains 148 matches, 2,698 stages, and 20,542 exchange units, with manually verified transcripts and segmentation. It preserves original stage scores, match votes, best-debater ballots, and adjudication rationales. We define three tasks: winner-tendency prediction, stage-score prediction, and best-debater prediction. Zero-shot evaluation of multiple large language models yields a highest winner-prediction accuracy of 66.2%, a highest Pearson correlation of 0.250 between model stage scores and mean human ratings, and a highest best-debater prediction accuracy of 56.8%. The dataset and benchmark provide a testbed for studying large language models' understanding of interactive argumentation and their agreement with professional judges.

</details>


### [49] [Analysing the Linearity of Linguistic Relations in Language Model Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21655)
*Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Fathima Thekkekara,John Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分析语言模型中不同语言关系在嵌入空间中线性编码强度的框架，通过约束线性近似方法在扩展的BATS数据集上测试GloVe、RoBERTa和ModernBERT，发现屈折和派生关系近乎完美线性编码，而词典和百科关系误差较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同语言关系在语言模型嵌入空间中线性编码的强度差异，以理解嵌入空间的关联结构可线性访问的程度。

Method: 将线性编码形式化为对相关与不相关词对的约束线性近似，并应用于扩展的BATS数据集，覆盖屈折、派生、词典及百科关系，在GloVe、RoBERTa和ModernBERT上进行实验。

Result: 屈折和派生关系呈现近乎完美的线性编码，词典和百科关系误差显著更高，尤其在一对多和多对多关联上；RoBERTa和ModernBERT通常比GloVe更线性地编码关系。

Conclusion: 该框架能揭示嵌入中最具线性可访问性的关系结构，为探测和比较不同模型的关系几何提供紧凑工具。

Abstract: We propose a framework to analyse how strongly different linguistic relations are linearly encoded in language model embedding spaces. We formalise linear encoding via a constrained linear approximation over related and unrelated word pairs and apply this to an extended BATS dataset covering inflectional, derivational, lexicographic, and encyclopedic relations in GloVe, RoBERTa, and ModernBERT. Our experiments show near-perfect linear encodings for inflectional and derivational relations, but substantially higher errors for lexicographic and encyclopedic relations, especially for one-to-many and many-to-many associations. We also find that RoBERTa and ModernBERT generally encode relations more linearly than GloVe. These results indicate that our framework can reveal which relational structures are most linearly accessible in embeddings, offering a compact tool for probing and comparing relational geometry across models.

</details>


### [50] [When Steering Fails in Latent Reasoning: A Latent-to-Language Transition Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21662)
*Gaoxiang Huang,Lei Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现激活引导（activation steering）在潜在思维链（latent CoT）中的效果显著弱于显式思维链（explicit CoT），并提出"潜在-语言转换鸿沟"来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导已被广泛用于在显式思维链推理中控制语言模型，研究者希望将其扩展到潜在思维链。但作者发现对连续思维（continuous thoughts）进行引导时，对后续语言生成的影响远弱于对显式思维链的引导，即使隐表示被移动的幅度相当。这一差异促使作者探究潜在空间干预为何无法有效传递到语言生成。

Method: 首先验证连续思维中仍可识别任务信息；随后提出"潜在-语言转换鸿沟"假设，即潜在空间的干预效果无法传递到语言生成。为验证该假设，作者进行两项分析：(1) 观察输出分布在转换边界处发生突变；(2) 比较任务相关方向在潜在思维链与显式思维链中的双向控制强度。

Result: 任务信息在连续思维中仍可被识别，说明信息本身并未丢失。输出分布在潜在到语言的转换边界处出现突变，且任务相关方向在潜在思维链中的双向控制效果远弱于显式思维链，支持"潜在-语言转换鸿沟"假设。

Conclusion: 转换接口（transition interface）是评估和设计未来潜在引导方法的核心目标；潜在空间与语言生成之间的转换鸿沟是限制潜在引导效果的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Activation steering has become a widely used approach for controlling language models during explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, motivating its extension to latent CoT. However, we find that steering continuous thoughts produces substantially weaker effects on subsequent language generation than steering explicit CoT, even when the hidden representations are moved by comparable amounts. We first show that task information remains identifiable in continuous thoughts. Hence, we hypothesize a \textbf{latent-to-language transition gap}, in which an intervention effect in latent space fails to transfer to language generation. Two further results support this hypothesis: the output distribution changes abruptly at the transition boundary, and task-related directions exert much weaker bidirectional control in latent CoT than in explicit CoT. These findings identify the transition interface as a central target for evaluating and designing future latent-steering methods.

</details>


### [51] [Rethinking Human-Aligned Evaluation: An Analysis of Semantic Metrics Beyond WER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21663)
*Hritika Sharma,Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Alessandra Pinto,Petr Motlicek,Hyunggu Jung,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Somang Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出英文人工评测数据集HATS-en，用于检验ASR转录质量指标与人类判断的一致性。结果显示WER一致性最低，SemDist最佳配置一致性最高，CER以低成本接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WER是ASR最常用的指标，但它将所有词汇偏差视为等价代价，忽略偏差是否改变语义，因此其是否能反映人类对转录质量的判断存疑，需要基于人类评测数据进行系统验证。

Method: 构建面向人工评测的英文数据集HATS-en；在该数据集上将词汇级指标与多种BERTScore、SemDist配置进行基准对比，配置维度包括语言模型、层选择与池化策略；并与CER、BERTScore等指标比较与人类判断的一致性。

Result: 在所有被测指标中WER与人类判断的一致性最低；最佳SemDist配置取得最高总体一致性，优于CER与BERTScore；不存在在所有设置下都最优的单一模型；CER虽然简单低成本，但其一致性与最优配置相当接近。

Conclusion: 支持将ASR评测转向CER（适用于英文及音节文字系统），因其更可解释、成本低且更贴近评测应捕捉的内容，同时将SemDist作为补充性评测指标。

Abstract: Word Error Rate (WER), the most commonly used metric for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), treats every lexical deviation from the reference as equally costly, regardless of whether it changes meaning. This raises the question: does WER actually track how humans judge ASR transcript quality? We introduce HATS-en, an English dataset for human-centered ASR evaluation. Using this dataset, we benchmark lexical metrics against several configurations of BERTScore and SemDist, varying the language model, layer, and pooling strategy. We find that WER agrees least with human judgment among all metrics tested, that the best-performing SemDist configurations achieve the highest overall agreement, ahead of CER and BERTScore, and that no single model is best across settings. CER, despite its simplicity and low cost, remains remarkably close to these best configurations. In line with prior recommendations, our results support shifting ASR evaluation toward CER both for English and for morphosyllabic writing systems as it is a more interpretable and low-cost metric for what evaluation should actually capture, and using SemDist as a complementary evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [PRISM-BN: A Controlled Corpus and Benchmark for Text-to-Parameterized Bayesian Network Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21673)
*Amartya Bhattacharya,Nikhil Singh,Neeti Pokhriyal,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRISM-BN 是一个包含 5054 条文本-BN 配对数据的受控语料库，配合 PRISM 边际优先构建流程，用于分离评估文本到贝叶斯网络的结构恢复与概率参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练文本到参数化贝叶斯网络（BN）系统需要大规模配对的文本-BN 资源，但此类资源在规模上不可得，因此缺乏可支撑神经符号 AI 研究的基准数据。

Method: 构建 PRISM-BN 受控语料库：5054 条 BN 描述配对离散参考 BN，涵盖变量、状态、有向边、根先验与完整多父节点 CPD，覆盖五个领域；实例源自 50 个 Wikipedia 种子骨架，概率为内部构造的基准目标。采用 PRISM 边际优先流程：先引出边际与局部联合分布，解析恢复归一化 CPD，并构建局部重参数化子图。基准评测含语义节点与状态对齐、条件结构打分及严格完整 CPD 评估。

Result: 在六个 LLM 抽取器上，Node F1 为 0.56–0.83，条件 Edge F1 为 0.90–0.97，CPD-KL 为 1.11–3.14。条件状态与边的恢复持续稳健，而严格完整 CPD 一致性仍具挑战。使用独立生成的 GPT-5.5 参考时趋势保持一致，人类试点也印证了结构可恢复性与相近的概率解释。

Conclusion: PRISM-BN 支持对结构恢复与概率参数估计进行分离评估，为神经符号文本到 BN 研究提供可控基准。

Abstract: Probabilistic Graphical Models (PGMs), especially Bayesian Networks (BNs), expose directed structure and probabilistic parameters, making them natural symbolic targets for neurosymbolic AI. Yet training text-to-parameterized-BN systems requires paired text-to-BN resources unavailable at scale. We introduce PRISM-BN, a controlled corpus of 5054 BN-grounded descriptions paired with discrete reference BNs containing variables, states, directed edges, root priors, and full multi-parent CPDs across five domains. The instances are derived from 50 Wikipedia-seeded backbones, and their probabilities are internally constructed benchmark targets rather than externally validated causal estimates. PRISM-BN is built with PRISM, a marginal-first pipeline that elicits marginal and local joint distributions, analytically recovers normalized CPDs, and constructs locally reparameterized subgraphs. We define a benchmark with semantic node and state alignment, conditional structural scoring, and strict full-CPD evaluation. Across six LLM extractors, Node F1 ranges from 0.56 to 0.83, conditional Edge F1 from 0.90 to 0.97, and CPD-KL from 1.11 to 3.14. Conditional state and edge recovery remain consistently strong, whereas strict full-CPD agreement remains challenging. These trends persist with independently generated GPT-5.5 references, and a human pilot corroborates structural recoverability and similar probabilistic interpretations. PRISM-BN supports separate evaluation of structural recovery and probabilistic parameter estimation.

</details>


### [53] [CIBuzzBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Understanding of Chinese Internet Buzzwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21722)
*Yifan Wang,Junyu Lu,Qifan Wang,Shun Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Jiahao Liu,Zhijun Cao,Lingbin Bu,Fanliang Bu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个中英跨语言中文网络流行语理解基准 CIBuzzBench，包含 3001 条流行语及英译解释、对等词、类别与有害性标注，设计三项评估任务，并评测主流专有与中文 LLM，发现模型在非字面解读、对等词匹配鲁棒性和有害性检测校准上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文社交媒体流行语含义多非字面，且植根于本土文化与语用语境；已有研究多局限于中文内部理解，尚未探究 LLM 能否跨语言迁移此类文化知识并准确传达英文含义。该跨语言能力对安全也至关重要，因为有害表达常借助文化特定的谐音、委婉、反讽或编码语言掩盖攻击性。

Method: 构建 CIBuzzBench：3001 条中文网络流行语，标注英文含义解释、英文对等表达、类别标签与有害性标签；基于标注设计三项任务——含义解释、跨语言对等词匹配、文化情境化有害性检测；在英文与中文提示两种设置下评测代表性前沿专有 LLM 与中文 LLM。

Result: LLM 在中文网络流行语的跨语言理解上仍表现不佳，尤其在细粒度非字面含义解释、选项扰动下的稳健对等词匹配、以及校准良好的有害性检测方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 结果凸显文化嵌入型语言现象对多语言 LLM 及面向安全的评估构成持续挑战；数据集与代码已开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: Chinese social media has generated a vast and continually evolving lexicon of internet buzzwords whose meanings are often non-literal and deeply rooted in local cultural and pragmatic contexts. Existing research has primarily focused on interpreting these buzzwords within Chinese, leaving largely unexplored whether LLMs can transfer such culturally grounded knowledge across languages and accurately convey the intended meanings in English. This cross-lingual capability is also critical for safety, as harmful expressions may obscure their offensive content through culture-specific homophony, euphemism, irony, or coded language. In this paper, we investigate the ability of advanced LLMs to understand Chinese internet buzzwords across languages. To this end, we introduce CIBuzzBench, the first benchmark for cross-lingual Chinese-to-English understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords. CIBuzzBench comprises 3,001 Chinese internet buzzwords annotated with English meaning explanations, English equivalents, category labels, and harmfulness labels. Based on these annotations, we design three evaluation tasks: Meaning Explanation, Cross-lingual Equivalent Matching, and Culturally Grounded Harmfulness Detection. We evaluate representative state-of-the-art proprietary and Chinese LLMs under both English- and Chinese-prompting settings. Our results show that LLMs continue to struggle with the cross-lingual understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords, particularly in fine-grained non-literal interpretation, robust equivalent matching under option perturbations, and calibrated harmfulness detection. These findings highlight the persistent challenges posed by culturally grounded language phenomena for multilingual LLMs and safety-oriented evaluation. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/SuperYFan/CIBuzzBench.

</details>


### [54] [Per-Aetiology Contrastive Severity Embeddings with Phonological Pseudo-Labelling for Multilingual Dysarthric Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21789)
*Bernard Muller,Antonio Armando Ortiz Barrañón,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在共享骨干、训练配方、语料注册表与留出评估下比较四种HuBERT-base对比嵌入模型：混合病因基线与CP、PD、ALS三个病因专属模型。结果显示病因专属模型的宏观F1全面优于混合基线（CP 0.829 vs 0.676，PD 0.715 vs 0.511，ALS 0.788 vs 0.596）；CP上加入伪标注说话人进一步将F1从0.786提升至0.829。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言构音障碍严重度系统通常只针对单一"病因—语言"对训练，或将异质病因合并进同一标签空间。作者旨在检验这种"合并"假设是否成立，即混合病因建模是否真的优于按病因单独建模。

Method: 构建四个匹配的HuBERT-base对比嵌入模型，共享骨干网络、训练配方、语料注册与留出评估：1个混合病因基线 + 3个病因专属模型（脑瘫CP、帕金森病PD、肌萎缩侧索硬化ALS）。训练结合临床标注语音与来自免训练音系刻画方法生成的序数伪标签。在说话人互斥、经泄漏过滤的留出子集上评估，各病因覆盖3至7种语言。

Result: 病因专属模型在三个目标病因上均超过混合基线：CP（宏观F1 0.829 vs 0.676，相对提升22.6%）、PD（0.715 vs 0.511，+40.0%）、ALS（0.788 vs 0.596，+32.3%）。在CP上，加入144名SAP与44名CDSD伪标注说话人后，宏观F1由0.786升至0.829，较仅用临床数据的CP模型提升4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究定位为对标签空间设计选择的受控比较，表明按病因分离标签空间优于混合建模。作者指出伪标签校准、数据划分卫生（泄漏控制）以及基于置信度阈值的部署仍是重要局限，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Most multilingual dysarthria-severity systems either train on a single aetiology-language pair or pool heterogeneous aetiologies into one label space. We test that pooling assumption with four matched HuBERT-base contrastive embedding models under a shared backbone, training recipe, corpus registry and held-out evaluation: one mixed-aetiology baseline and three aetiology-specific models for cerebral palsy (CP), Parkinson's disease (PD) and amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Training combines clinically labelled speech with ordinal pseudo-labels from a training-free phonological profiling method [1], [2]. On speaker-disjoint, leakage-filtered held-out subsets, the per-aetiology models outperform the mixed baseline across all three target aetiologies: CP (macro F1 0.829 vs 0.676, +22.6 % relative), PD (0.715 vs 0.511, +40.0 %) and ALS (0.788 vs 0.596, +32.3 %). On CP, adding 144 SAP and 44 CDSD pseudo-labelled speakers lifts macro F1 from 0.786 to 0.829 over a clinical-only CP model (+4.3 percentage points). Training data span three to seven languages per aetiology. We position this as a controlled comparison of label-space design choices and discuss pseudo-label calibration, split hygiene, and confidence-thresholded deployment as important limitations for future work.

</details>


### [55] [RheoSampling: Resolving the One-Hot Dilemma in Stochastic Dynamic-Tree Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21827)
*Qiao Hu,Yepeng Weng,Bo Zhang,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RheoSampling，通过解耦"树构建"与"token 验证"两个角色的概率使用，使动态树投机解码在随机采样（T>0）下同时保持上下文感知的 top-K 结构与无损的随机采样能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态树方法（如 EAGLE-3）在贪婪解码下依赖确定性 top-K 扩展与全局剪枝，表现优异；但在随机解码（T>0）时该机制会将草稿分布退化为 one-hot，导致接受率大幅下降。由此形成两难：动态树牺牲随机采样以保留上下文相关拓扑，静态树保留随机采样却使用上下文无关结构。根本原因是同一概率分布被同时用于两个相互冲突的任务——构建树与验证 token，这种耦合使得直接注入随机性变得困难。

Method: 提出 RheoSampling，将两个角色解耦：对从草稿分布中采样得到的 token 赋予一个代理概率（proxy probability）用于树的扩展与剪枝，同时保留其真实采样概率用于验证。具体做法是在确定性 top-K 槽位中注入一个采样 token，并在构建与验证阶段对其使用不同概率，从而成为首个兼具上下文感知 top-K 构建与随机采样且保持无损性的动态树方法。通过等价类分析将随机树空间压缩为可处理的类别，建立无损性保证；并引入基于最优传输（OT）的验证策略与稀疏草稿机制，使理论收益转化为实际效率。

Result: 在多种 LLM 与基准测试上，RheoSampling 相比当前最先进的动态树方法在接受率与加速比上均有提升。

Conclusion: 该工作通过解耦树构建与 token 验证的概率角色，解决了动态树投机解码在随机采样下的接受率崩塌问题，同时保持无损性；其等价类分析框架可为随机树结构的分析提供通用模板。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel, with tree-based methods further improving efficiency through hierarchical structures. Dynamic-tree methods such as EAGLE-3 perform well under greedy decoding via deterministic top-K expansion and global pruning. However, in stochastic decoding (T>0), this mechanism collapses the draft distribution into one-hot probabilities, causing a severe drop in acceptance rate. This creates a dilemma: dynamic-tree methods sacrifice stochastic sampling to preserve context-aware topology, while static-tree methods preserve stochastic sampling with context-agnostic structures. The issue arises because the same probability distribution is used for two conflicting tasks: constructing the tree and verifying tokens. This coupling makes direct injection of randomness challenging due to the resulting stochastic process. We resolve this by decoupling these roles: RheoSampling assigns a token sampled from the draft distribution a proxy probability for tree expansion and pruning alongside its true sampling probability for verification. Specifically, we inject a sampled token among the deterministic top-K slots and treat it with different probabilities during construction and verification, making RheoSampling the first dynamic-tree method with both context-aware top-K construction and stochastic sampling while maintaining losslessness. We establish the lossless guarantee through an equivalence-class analysis that compresses the stochastic tree space into tractable classes. An OT-based verification strategy and a sparse draft mechanism ensure that theoretical gains translate into practical efficiency. Experiments across LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate improvements in acceptance rate and speedup over state-of-the-art dynamic tree methods. This framework may provide a template for analyzing stochastic tree structures.

</details>


### [56] [Reusing Latent Speech Representations for Query-Conditioned Topic Localization in Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21844)
*Steffen Freisinger,Philipp Seeberger,Thomas Ranzenberger,Tobias Bocklet,Korbinian Riedhammer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出查询条件下的主题定位任务，复用ASR编码器状态作为句级表示，与文本嵌入融合，使轻量级跨度定位器可利用语音信息，在两个公开数据集上稳定优于纯文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长转录文本作为下游NLP输入成本高且含大量无关内容，需要定位与主题标题查询最相关的句子跨度；同时避免为利用语音信息而额外运行独立音频编码器。

Method: 查询条件下的主题定位（预测转录中最佳回答主题标题查询的句子跨度）；复用ASR编码器状态作为句级表示，并与文本嵌入融合，供轻量级跨度定位器使用。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上相较纯文本基线取得一致性提升，在严格边界匹配标准下尤为明显；跨数据集实验表明，结构化或半结构化语音收益最强，自发语音收益有限且不一致。

Conclusion: 复用ASR编码器状态融合文本嵌入可低成本引入语音信息并提升跨度定位，但其有效性依赖语音类型，对自发语音的泛化收益有限。

Abstract: Long transcripts are costly inputs for downstream NLP systems and often contain irrelevant context. We study query-conditioned topic localization: predicting the sentence span in a transcript that best addresses a topic-title query. To improve span localization, we reuse ASR encoder states as sentence-level representations and fuse them with textual embeddings. This lets lightweight span locators exploit speech information without running a separate audio encoder. Experiments on two public datasets show consistent gains over text-only baselines, especially under strict boundary-matching criteria. Cross-dataset experiments further indicate that the benefits are strongest for structured or semi-structured speech, while gains on spontaneous speech are limited and mixed.

</details>


### [57] [Do Personality-Tuned LLMs Make Better Social Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21857)
*Tim Krabbe,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察人格感知微调能否提升LLM人格化对话生成的一致性与可控性，结果显示微调模型在扮演不同人格方面并未优于基线模型，且评分者间一致性偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于社会模拟中的社交交互智能体与机器人，虽能很好地模仿人类行为，但仍存在一种持续的"异质感"。作者希望探究人格感知微调是否相较仅用指令提示，能通过提升人格条件对话生成的一致性与可控性来缩小这一差距。

Method: 对两个小型开源权重LLM（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Ministral-8B-Instruct）进行微调，训练语料结合了带人格标签的社交媒体帖文与对话，构建面向社会模拟的人格化对话引擎。随后在多种社交互动场景中评估所得模型，由三个独立LLM评审员评定人格保真度并给出现据支持的行为解读，同时量化评分者间一致性与生成对话的词汇特征。

Result: 微调模型在角色扮演不同人格方面并不优于各自的基线模型；但评分者间一致性较低，限制了该结论的可信度。在生成文本质量上，微调模型大体与基线相当，其中微调提升了Qwen模型的语言多样性。

Conclusion: 结果总体具备可用性，基线模型表现最佳；未来研究应更重视训练数据的质量与领域对齐，以实现准确的人格角色扮演。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used in social simulations for socially interactive agents and robots, offering more flexibility than rule-based systems. However, even though they mimic human behaviour very well, there is a persistent alienness to them. This work investigates whether personality-aware fine-tuning can reduce this gap by improving the consistency and controllability of personality-conditioned dialogue generation compared with instruction prompting alone. We fine-tune two small open-weight LLMs, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Ministral-8B-Instruct, using a corpus that combines personality-labelled social media posts and dialogues to create a personality-based dialogue engine for social simulation. The resulting models are evaluated across multiple social interaction scenarios using three independent LLM judges, which assess personality fidelity and provide evidence-based behavioral interpretations. We additionally quantify inter-rater agreement and lexical characteristics of the generated dialogue. Results indicate that fine-tuned models are not better at role-playing different personalities than their respective baseline models. However, low inter-rater agreement limits the confidence with which these results can be interpreted. Concerning the quality of generated texts, fine-tuned models are mostly comparable to the baselines, with fine-tuning improving the linguistic diversity of the Qwen models. While the results appear generally usable and the baseline models offer the best overall performance, future studies should place greater emphasis on the quality and domain alignment of training data for accurate personality role-playing.

</details>


### [58] [TrialAtlas: Multi-Agent Research Organization for Clinical Trial Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21859)
*Jiacheng Lin,Zifeng Wang,Zheng Chen,Erick Scott,Ziwei Yang,Fanyang Yu,Sheng Zhong,Jimeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TrialAtlas：记忆增强的多智能体研究组织，用于临床开发规划（CDP），通过协调文献综合、竞争试验情报、监管先例分析和整合推理等专用智能体，模拟专家协作过程，并从历史临床试验与监管结果中学习；配套构建 TrialAtlasBench（基于 291 份 FDA 完整回复函），在缺陷检测、设计改进建议和技术与监管成功预测三类任务上均优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近 90% 进入临床开发的药物最终失败，药企虽依赖临床开发规划（CDP）与技术及监管成功概率评估来预判开发风险，但此类决策高度依赖人力且主观性强，需临床科学、统计、监管事务与竞争情报等多领域专家共同获取、综合并推理异构证据，成本高、可扩展性差。因此需要一种能模拟该协作流程、并以历史开发经验为基础的自动化方法。

Method: 提出 TrialAtlas——记忆增强的多智能体（multi-agent）研究组织，通过协调多个专用智能体分别负责文献综合、竞争性试验情报、监管先例分析，以及针对试验设计与开发风险的整合推理；同时从历史临床试验与监管结果（包括既往新药申请 NDA）中学习，将决策建立在累积的开发经验之上。为在真实监管场景中评估，构建 TrialAtlasBench，基于 291 份 FDA 完整回复函（Complete Response Letters），覆盖三类任务：识别试验设计缺陷、推荐可执行的设计改进、预测技术与监管成功。

Result: 缺陷检测 F1 达 50.0%，较最强基线提升 6.1 个百分点；技术与监管成功预测达 85.3% 平衡准确率和 84.7% F1，较最佳基线在平衡准确率上提升 6.7 个百分点、在 Cohen's kappa 上提升 12.0 个百分点。专家评估中，TrialAtlas 生成的问题 86.4% 被判定有效，对比 OpenAI DeepResearch 为 83.1%、Gemini DeepResearch 为 59.3%。

Conclusion: TrialAtlas 通过记忆增强的多智能体协作与历史监管经验学习，能够在真实监管场景下有效支持临床开发规划，在缺陷检测与成功预测任务上稳定超越强基线，并生成更高质量、经专家认可的分析结论，为降低药物临床开发风险与主观决策偏差提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Nearly 90% of drugs entering clinical development ultimately fail, despite billions of dollars in investment. Pharmaceutical companies therefore rely on clinical development planning (CDP) and probability of technical and regulatory success assessment to anticipate development risks, yet these decisions remain labor-intensive and subjective, requiring experts across clinical science, statistics, regulatory affairs, and competitive intelligence to jointly acquire, synthesize, and reason over heterogeneous evidence. Here, we introduce TrialAtlas, a memory-augmented multi-agent research organization for CDP that mirrors this collaborative process by coordinating specialized agents for literature synthesis, competitive trial intelligence, regulatory precedent analysis, and integrated reasoning over trial design and development risk. TrialAtlas further learns from historical clinical trials and regulatory outcomes, including prior New Drug Applications (NDAs), to ground its decisions in accumulated development experience. To evaluate these capabilities in an authentic regulatory setting, we introduce TrialAtlasBench, constructed from 291 FDA Complete Response Letters and spanning three practical tasks: detecting trial design deficiencies, recommending actionable design improvements, and predicting technical and regulatory success. TrialAtlas achieves an F1 score of 50.0% for deficiency detection, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.1 points, and reaches 85.3% balanced accuracy and 84.7% F1 for prediction of technical and regulatory success, improving over the best baselines by 6.7 points in balanced accuracy and 12.0 points in Cohen's kappa. In expert evaluation, 86.4% of TrialAtlas-generated concerns were judged valid, compared with 83.1% for OpenAI DeepResearch and 59.3% for Gemini DeepResearch.

</details>


### [59] [NemotronLabs VoiceChat: An Open Full-duplex Speech-to-Speech Model with Tool Calling Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21967)
*Jagadeesh Balam,Travis Bartley,Edresson Casanova,Sanjay Chauhan,Chen Chen,Zhehuai Chen,Zijia Chen,Francesco Ciannella,Slyne Deng,Mikyas Desta,Harishchandra Dubey,Slim Essid,Nourchene Ferchichi,Boris Ginsburg,Mariana Graterol Fuenmayor,Negar Habibi,Kevin Hu,Anand Joseph,Viraj Karandikar,Myungjong Kim,Viacheslav Klimkov,Seelan Lakshmi Narasimhan,Lily Lee,Jason Li,Eileen Long,Ameya Mahabaleshwarkar,Aditya Malte,Adi Margolin,Sasha Meister,Valentin Mendelev,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Ankita Pasad,Yifan Peng,Elena Rastorgueva,Jayda Ritchie,Jason Roche,Nikhil Srihari,Yuanhang Su,Yoshi Suhara,Viet Anh Trinh,Jinhan Wang,Piotr Zelasko,Hui Wang,Puhui Meng,Chaosen Zhang,Yunsheng Liu,Shawn Wang,Wenjing Li,Zhonglei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NemotronLabs VoiceChat：开源全双工语音到语音模型，原生支持工具调用，在多个全双工基准上取得领先或竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对话系统通常割裂处理全双工交互、语音识别与生成、语言推理及外部工具调用，难以在统一流式架构中兼顾自然对话所需的时序行为。本文旨在构建一个可同时整合这些能力且保持实时对话特性的开源语音到语音模型。

Method: 模型由流式语音编码器与仅解码器语言模型组成，配备并行的专用输出流分别生成智能体文本和结构化函数调用，另设辅助RNN-T分支进行增量用户转写，并接入流式TTS解码器。整体在统一流式架构内实现倾听、转写、推理、调用工具与发声。

Result: 在Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0上，其暂停处理接管率为评估的开源权重系统中最低，用户打断后接管率达100%，打断后响应质量得分4.33/5；在Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5上，用户反馈后恢复响应的比例为93%；VoiceBench归一化平均分55.1；在Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0上工具选择F1为82.5%，但参数准确率和端到端工具执行仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 结果表明，全双工交互、语音识别与生成、通用语言能力及外部工具使用可集成于单一开源语音到语音模型中，且不牺牲实时对话行为，为该方向提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We introduce NemotronLabs VoiceChat, an open full-duplex speech-to-speech model with native tool-calling capabilities. NemotronLabs VoiceChat combines a streaming speech encoder and decoder-only language model with parallel specialized output streams for agent text and structured function calls, an auxiliary RNN-T branch for incremental user transcription, and a streaming TTS decoder. This design enables the model to listen, transcribe, reason, invoke tools, and speak within a unified streaming architecture while preserving the temporal behavior required for natural conversation. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0, NemotronLabs VoiceChat achieves the lowest pause-handling takeover rates among evaluated open-weight systems, 100\% takeover following user interruptions, and a 4.33/5 post-interruption response-quality score. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5, it resumes its response after user backchannels in 93\% of cases. NemotronLabs VoiceChat obtains a 55.1 normalized average on VoiceBench and, on Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0 (FDB 3.0), achieves 82.5\% tool-selection F1, while argument accuracy and end-to-end tool execution remain areas for improvement. These results demonstrate that full-duplex interaction, speech recognition and generation, general language capabilities, and external tool use can be integrated in a single open speech-to-speech model without sacrificing real-time conversational behavior.

</details>


### [60] [Moral Entropy: Auditing Bias and Uncertainty in Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21992)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Moral Entropy 贝叶斯框架，将道德标注分歧建模为可学习的不确定性，而非直接投票消除；通过熵分解与校准评估审计常见聚合规则，揭示其系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算伦理研究通常将标注者对道德内容的分歧视为噪声，采用多数投票或"任一标注者标注即通过"（any-annotator）规则进行消解。作者认为这种不确定性应被显式建模和学习，而非被抹平，因为常见聚合规则可能引入未被报告的偏差。

Method: 提出 Moral Entropy，一个贝叶斯框架：维持对真实标签的完整后验分布，并将熵分解为偶然不确定性（关于道德内容不可约的分歧）与认知不确定性（源于标注不足或噪声）；同时利用交叉熵/KL、Brier 分数、期望校准误差等熵方法，将任意启发式共识规则与校准后的真值进行对照审计。

Result: 在三个语料库、十五个话语领域上审计标准聚合规则，发现现有流程未报告的偏差：any-annotator 规则在约 30% 的条目上与校准后验不一致，汇总后几乎全为假阳性，但在 foundation 层面错误发生反转（MFTC 上平均 FPR/FNR 为 19.9%/38.9%）；较严格的多数投票与两票规则则漏掉 63-83% 的真阳性。

Conclusion: 道德标注中的分歧不应被简单投票消除，而应作为不确定性被建模与学习。Moral Entropy 提供了一种审计共识规则偏差的校准化方法，并揭示常用规则在假阳性与假阴性上的系统性取舍。

Abstract: Most work in computational ethics treats annotator disagreement on moral content as noise to be voted away, collapsed into majority vote or the more permissive any-annotator rule the moment a single annotator flags an item. We argue this uncertainty should instead be modeled and learned from.
  We introduce Moral Entropy, a Bayesian framework that keeps a full posterior over the true label and decomposes its entropy into aleatoric uncertainty (irreducible disagreement about the moral content) and epistemic uncertainty (from insufficient or noisy annotation) -- and lets any heuristic consensus rule be audited against a calibrated ground truth via entropy methods such as cross-entropy/KL, Brier score, and expected calibration error.
  Across three corpora and fifteen discourse domains, auditing the standard aggregation rules against this posterior reveals bias that no current pipeline reports: the any-annotator rule disagrees with the calibrated posterior on roughly 30% of items -- pooled, almost entirely false positives, though the errors invert at the foundation level (19.9%/38.9% mean FPR/FNR on MFTC) -- while the stricter majority and two-vote rules miss 63-83% of true positives.

</details>


### [61] [RecreationWorld: Scalable and Verifiable Environments for Hybrid Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22000)
*Shuai Bai,Jiayong Deng,Yikun Fu,Chang Gao,Xuhao Hu,Mianqiu Huang,Yizhen Jiang,Yuheng Jing,Dehui Kong,Keliang Li,Ning Li,Wanli Li,Dayiheng Liu,Dunjie Lu,Changwei Luo,Que Shen,Zheyuan Wang,Zijian Wang,Jie Wu,Gao Wu,Zhihui Xie,Rui Xie,Haiyang Xu,An Yang,Jiakang Yuan,Yanming Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Xi Zhang,Zhenru Zhang,Zhuo Zhen,Mingkang Zhu,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出混合型计算机使用智能体（hybrid CUA）任务：智能体需在探索界面与编写代码之间自主切换，基于运行中的参考实现重建功能一致的软件。作者构建了跨 Ubuntu、macOS、Windows、Android、Web 五平台的 RecreationWorld 框架与统一工具链，以参考实现作为隐藏行为测试的 oracle 提供执行接地奖励；据此生成轨迹并训练模型。为离线评测，作者发布 RecreationBench（250 个跨域跨平台任务，含程序化与视觉断言）。GPT-6 Astra 总体得分 58.1%，但仅 2.8% 任务通过全部程序化测试，显示模型更擅长复现静态界面结构而非交互与计算输出，且生成应用规模更小、更单体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体分两条独立路线：图形界面交互，以及通过代码/命令行进行软件开发。但真实数字工作需要在二者之间交织往返，而非端到端串接。因此需要研究能在探索界面、实现软件、运行并视觉验证产物之间自主决策的混合型智能体，并提供可复现、执行接地的评测手段。

Method: 提出 RecreationWorld：一个以"复刻（recreation）"为核心任务的五平台框架。给定一个运行中的参考应用，智能体需自行发现其行为并构建忠实实现，无预设工作流。框架提供 Ubuntu、macOS、Windows、Android、Web 的可复现环境，以及统一 harness，支持原生 GUI 控制与编码工具。参考实现作为隐藏行为测试的 oracle，提供执行接地奖励。利用高质量开源应用扩展轨迹生成规模并训练模型。离线评测提出 RecreationBench：250 个跨域跨平台任务，采用参考接地的程序化断言与视觉断言，覆盖多种交互深度下的动作条件化结果，每条断言先经参考实现与人工评审验证，再冻结用于自动评分。

Result: 基于轨迹训练的模型在五个分布外编码与混合计算机使用基准上均有提升，并更频繁地对渲染输出进行视觉验证，表明存在超出复刻任务的迁移能力。评测中 GPT-6 Astra 总体得分最高，为 58.1%，但仅 2.8% 的任务通过全部程序化测试。智能体复现静态界面结构比复现交互与计算输出更可靠；生成应用相比参考实现更小、更单体化。

Conclusion: 混合型 CUA 具备现实必要性，但现有模型能力仍明显不足，尤其体现在交互逻辑与计算正确性上，而非界面外观。RecreationWorld 与 RecreationBench 提供了执行接地、可复现的评测基础设施，作者开源基准、环境与测试套件，以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have advanced along two separate lines: graphical interaction and software development through code and the command line. Real digital work requires both, interleaved rather than stacked end to end. We study hybrid CUAs that autonomously decide when to explore an interface, implement software, and run and visually verify their artifacts. We introduce RecreationWorld, a five-platform framework built around recreation: given a running reference, an agent must discover its behavior and build a faithful implementation with no prescribed workflow. RecreationWorld provides reproducible environments on Ubuntu, macOS, Windows, Android, and Web, plus a unified harness with native GUI control and coding tools. The running reference serves as an oracle for hidden behavioral tests, providing execution-grounded rewards. We scale trajectory generation with high-quality open-source applications. Models trained on these trajectories improve across five out-of-distribution coding and hybrid computer-use benchmarks and more frequently verify their rendered outputs, providing evidence of transfer beyond recreation. For held-out evaluation, we introduce RecreationBench, comprising 250 diverse tasks across domains and platforms. Reference-grounded programmatic and visual assertions cover action-conditioned outcomes at multiple interaction depths; each is validated on the reference and by human reviewers before the suite is frozen for automatic scoring. GPT-6 Astra leads at 58.1% overall, but passes all programmatic tests on just 2.8% of tasks. Agents reproduce static interface structure more reliably than interactions and computed outputs, while generated applications remain smaller and more monolithic than their references. We release the benchmark, environments, and test suites.

</details>


### [62] [DiaVLo: Diagnosing Behaviours of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22008)
*Lorenzo Corti,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiaVLo：一个利用人工筛选与VLM生成能力构建行为规范并定位关键概念的视觉语言模型诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）依赖子组件间信息的存储与传递，验证其是否表现出期望行为、避免有害行为对其可靠部署至关重要。然而，能够识别VLM行为的方法仍然匮乏。

Method: 提出DiaVLo诊断框架：借助人工筛选（human curation）与VLM自身的生成能力，构建关于期望行为与观测行为的规范（specifications），从而揭示潜在的错位（misalignment）；此外还提供因果估计，以识别对VLM行为影响最大的关键概念（concepts）。

Result: 在多个开源VLM上、在分类与生成两种条件下进行评估：DiaVLo生成的行为标签与模型性能相关，并为测量到的性能提供上下文；同时揭示了明显对齐与错位的行为，以及VLM在概念感知、组织和优先级排序方面的模式。

Conclusion: DiaVLo能够系统性地刻画VLM行为，识别对齐/错位现象，并通过因果估计定位影响行为的关键概念，为VLM的可靠部署提供诊断支持。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on storing and transferring appropriate information across their sub-components. Verifying that the VLMs exhibit desired behaviours, while avoiding harmful ones, is central to their reliable deployment. Yet, methods that identify VLM behaviours remain scarce. We present DiaVLo, a diagnostic framework that leverages human curation and VLMs' generation capabilities to construct specifications of desired and observed VLM behaviours, surfacing potential misalignments. Beyond this, DiaVLo also provides causal estimates to identify the most influential concepts steering VLM behaviours. We evaluate DiaVLo on several open-source VLMs under both classification and generation conditions. Our experiments show that DiaVLo produces behaviour labels that correlate with model performance and provide context for measured performance. DiaVLo surfaced behaviours that are clearly aligned and misaligned, alongside patterns in how VLMs perceive, organise, and prioritise concepts.

</details>


### [63] [QuranicMMLU: A Cognitively-Aware Benchmark for Evaluating Generative AI Solutions on Quranic Linguistic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22038)
*Rawan El Ghali,Umm Kulsoom,Anas Madkoor,Dima Faris Alsaudi,Roaa Abdelmagid,Roaa Ibrahim,Raghad Mousa,Hamza Aljaji,Abdullah Khanafer,Abdallah Alkanani,Salah Feras Alali,Rawan Khaled Mohamed,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QuranicMMLU，一个面向《古兰经》阿拉伯语的多维语言学评测基准，包含五支柱分类体系与980道经人工审核的双形式（开放式与多选题）题目，用于评估生成式AI的语言学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有《古兰经》相关基准主要聚焦通用问答与语义检索，未能针对具体语言学能力进行探测，也未按认知需求与经文难度进行分层，因此缺乏对阿拉伯语NLP在《古兰经》领域能力的细粒度、语言学基础的评估手段。

Method: 构建涵盖音系学、形态学、句法学、语义学与语用学的五支柱分类体系，共31个叶子节点，涉及tajwīd、词根-模式形态、启示场合与跨章连贯等现象；为每个叶子节点生成按Bloom认知层级与经文困惑度分层的题目，采用LLM作为评判者独立作答与评分并将标注送交人工复核；最终形成980道人工审核题目，每道题同时以开放式与多选题两种形式呈现，并在12个系统上进行基准测试。

Result: 伊斯兰专用模型表现最佳；所有系统多选题准确率（平均84%）均高于开放式答案质量（平均60%）；两种形式的系统排名高度一致（Kendall's τ=0.73），但多选题评分掩盖了在移除选项后才显现的失败。

Conclusion: QuranicMMLU为《古兰经》领域的阿拉伯语NLP评估提供了严谨且具语言学基础的框架。

Abstract: We introduce QuranicMMLU, a benchmark for evaluating generative AI on Quranic Arabic across multiple dimensions of linguistic complexity. Existing Quranic benchmarks center on general question answering and semantic retrieval, without probing specific linguistic competencies or stratifying by cognitive demand and verse difficulty. We construct a five-pillar Quranic taxonomy spanning Phonology, Morphology, Syntax, Semantics, and Pragmatics, with 31 leaves covering phenomena from tajwīd and root-and-pattern morphology to occasions of revelation and inter-surah coherence. For each leaf we generate questions stratified by Bloom's cognitive level and verse perplexity, then have LLM as a judge to independently answer and score every item and route the annotations to manual review. The resulting dataset comprises 980 human-reviewed questions, each issued in both open-ended and multiple-choice form. We benchmark 12 systems on these items and find that the Islamic-specialized model leads, yet every system scores higher on multiple-choice accuracy (average 84%) than open-ended answer quality (average 60%): the two rankings agree closely (Kendall's τ=0.73), but multiple-choice scoring hides failures that surface only once answer choices are removed. QuranicMMLU thus offers a rigorous, linguistically grounded framework for evaluating Arabic NLP in the Quranic domain.

</details>


### [64] [An Interpretable Memory Decision Controller for LLM Agents Based on Three-Signal Complementarity: Decoupling Confidence and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22043)
*Yiming Zhang,Jinghong Zhang,Haoran Zhao,Yiren Ma,Chunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆决策层（MDL），一个位于检索与生成之间、零参数的记忆决策控制器，通过融合相关性、可靠性与任务风险的三信号互补编码器，显式解耦置信度与一致性，并引入风险反转与显式弃答，显著降低冲突记忆下的幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向大语言模型的记忆系统主要关注高效检索，而"是否应信任被检索到的记忆"这一问题研究甚少。当记忆库中存在冲突立场时，标准RAG会盲目注入记忆并放大幻觉：在易受记忆注入影响的模型中，冲突记忆下的RAG幻觉率明显高于无记忆基线。

Method: 受前额叶皮层记忆信号机制启发，提出记忆决策层（MDL）：一个置于检索与生成阶段之间的零参数记忆决策控制器。核心是基于QR的正交子空间投影与元工作记忆信号构成的三信号互补编码器，将相关性、可靠性与任务风险融合为可解释的决策表示，用以量化检索记忆的可信度；在此基础上显式解耦置信度与一致性，并引入风险反转与显式弃答。

Result: 在主流大语言模型与多个开源数据集上的评估显示，MDL在一般场景下将冲突记忆下的幻觉率降低约56.04%，在高风险场景下接近零幻觉。控制器完全白盒，仅依赖几何运算，无需训练参数，每次决策仅增加约0.14 ms，比其前置的嵌入检索步骤快约50倍，比一次LLM自评估调用快四到五个数量级。

Conclusion: 将记忆可信度决策显式建模为检索与生成之间的轻量控制环节，可在零参数、可解释、极低延迟的条件下有效抑制冲突记忆引发的幻觉，为记忆增强生成系统提供了新的可信度控制范式。

Abstract: Memory systems for large language models have focused predominantly on efficient retrieval, whereas the decision of whether retrieved memories should be trusted has received comparatively little attention. When the memory store contains conflicting positions, standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) blindly injects memories and amplifies hallucinations: in models susceptible to memory injection, the RAG hallucination rate under conflicting memories is markedly higher than that of a memory-free baseline. Inspired by memory signaling mechanisms in the prefrontal cortex, we propose the Memory Decision Layer (MDL), a zero-parameter memory decision controller situated between the retrieval and generation stages. Its core is a three-signal complementary encoder that fuses relevance, reliability, and task risk through QR-based orthogonal subspace projection and a meta-working-memory signal into an interpretable decision representation that quantifies the trustworthiness of retrieved memories. Building on this encoder, MDL explicitly decouples confidence from consistency and introduces risk inversion and explicit abstention. Evaluations on mainstream large language models and multiple open-source datasets show that MDL reduces the hallucination rate under conflicting memories by about 56.04% in general scenarios and approaches zero hallucination in high-risk scenarios. The controller is fully white-box: it relies purely on geometric operations, requires no trained parameters, and adds only about 0.14 ms per decision -- roughly 50x faster than the embedding-retrieval step that precedes it and four to five orders of magnitude faster than an LLM self-evaluation call.

</details>


### [65] [Cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in occupational accident narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22081)
*Aho Yapi,Pierre Latouche,Arnaud Guillin,Yan Bailly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估法文职业事故叙述中"事故过程角色分类"的跨行业泛化能力：在建筑行业数据上训练角色分类器，直接迁移至冶金、化学-塑料及独立企业语料，发现任务特定适配优于冻结预训练表示，三种最优策略平均平衡准确率达85.6%–85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业事故叙述蕴含工作情境、不利条件、事故事件及后果等关键信息，自动结构化可支撑大规模事故分析与职业风险预防。但不同行业与组织在术语和写作风格上差异显著，自动编码系统能否泛化到训练域之外尚不明确，故需评估跨行业泛化能力。

Method: 构建专家标注语料，将事实单元划分为四类角色：工作情境(A0)、明确报告的不利条件(A1)、事故事件或偏差(B)、报告的后果(C)。分类器仅在建筑行业6,040篇叙述抽取的42,244个事实单元上开发与选择，随后在不重训练、不做目标域调优的前提下，于冶金、化学-塑料及独立企业语料上评估。对比冻结预训练表示、任务特定微调与监督表示学习策略。

Result: 任务特定适配在跨域迁移上一致优于冻结表示。多次训练运行中，三种领先的任务适配策略在三个目标语料上的平均平衡准确率介于85.6%至85.8%之间。

Conclusion: 研究支持开发可迁移的辅助编码系统，能够一致地结构化异构职业事故叙述，服务于专家复核与跨行业预防分析。

Abstract: Occupational accident narratives contain valuable information about work situations, unfavourable conditions, accident events, and their consequences. Automatically structuring these narratives can facilitate large-scale accident analysis and support occupational risk prevention. However, the terminology and writing styles used to describe accidents vary considerably across sectors and organisations, raising questions about the ability of automated coding systems to generalize beyond their training domain. In this paper, we evaluate the cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in French occupational accident narratives. We construct an expert-annotated corpus in which factual units are classified into four roles: work situation (A0), explicitly reported unfavourable condition (A1), accident event or deviation (B), and reported consequence (C). The role classifiers are developed and selected exclusively on 42,244 factual units extracted from 6,040 construction-sector narratives and are then evaluated on unseen corpora from the metallurgy and chemistry--plastics sectors, as well as on an independently collected company corpus, without retraining or target-domain tuning of the role classifier. We compare frozen pretrained representations with task-specific fine-tuning and supervised representation-learning strategies. The results show that task-specific adaptation consistently improves cross-domain transfer over frozen representations. Across repeated training runs, the three leading task-adapted strategies achieved average balanced accuracies between 85.6% and 85.8% across the three target corpora. These findings support the development of transferable assisted-coding systems capable of consistently structuring heterogeneous occupational accident narratives for expert review and cross-sector prevention analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [66] [BirdsongChat: A Hybrid Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Embodied Behavior Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20887)
*Callie C. Liao,Duoduo Liao,Ellie L. Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出基于统一参数表示（UPR）的混合多智能体框架，用LLM推理智能体将多模态输入转为可解释控制参数，驱动仿真智能体生成同步的3D运动、空间化声景与环境行为，并以BirdsongChat鸟类行为仿真原型验证其跨模态一致性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态具身系统需将人类意图转化为跨异构模态的可解释、协调行为，但现有多模态智能体多依赖隐式表示，导致可控性受限、跨模态一致性不足，缺乏语义推理与物理执行之间的显式桥梁。

Method: 提出混合多智能体框架，核心为统一参数表示（UPR）：LLM推理智能体把文本/图像等多模态输入解析为编码行为状态与可解释控制参数的UPR；仿真智能体据此生成同步的3D运动、空间化声音与环境行为。以BirdsongChat为原型，在交互式鸟类行为仿真平台上耦合鸣声、发声与环境语境，并在物种、行为、情感状态、环境及多鸟交互场景下评估。

Result: 跨模态一致性归一化得分94.4%，情感一致性100%，生成一致性92.6%。

Conclusion: 显式中间表示能有效衔接语义推理与物理执行，提升可控性与多模态同步性；该框架为需可解释“语义—物理”协调的具身AI提供通用设计原则，可应用于生物启发生态声学、群体机器人、虚拟环境与创意多媒体等领域。

Abstract: Multimodal embodied systems require translating human intentions into interpretable and coordinated behaviors across heterogeneous modalities. However, existing multimodal agents often rely on implicit representations, limiting controllability and cross-modal consistency. We present a hybrid multi-agent framework for interactive multimodal behavior simulation that bridges semantic reasoning and physical execution through a Unified Parameter Representation (UPR). LLM-based reasoning agents transform multimodal inputs into UPR, which encodes behavioral states and interpretable control parameters for simulation agents generating synchronized 3D motion, spatialized soundscapes, and environmental behaviors. We develop BirdsongChat as a prototype implementation of the proposed framework, using interactive avian behavior simulation as a testbed that tightly couples motion, vocalization, and environmental context. BirdsongChat is evaluated on text- and image-guided scenarios involving species, behaviors, affective states, environments, and multi-bird interactions. The system achieves normalized scores of 94.4\% for cross-modal coherence, 100% for affective consistency, and 92.6% for generation consistency. These results demonstrate that an explicit intermediate representation effectively bridges semantic reasoning and physical execution, improving controllability and multimodal synchronization. The proposed framework thus offers a generalizable design principle for embodied AI systems requiring interpretable semantic-to-physical coordination across modalities, with potential applications in bio-inspired ecoacoustics, swarm robotics, virtual environments, and creative multimedia.

</details>


### [67] [Proxifield: Decentralized Multi-Agent Communication through Semantic Proximity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20889)
*Pradyumna Tambwekar,Yenchia Feng,Deep Patel,Karime Maamari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Proxifield：一种基于语义邻近度构建稀疏通信图的轮次自适应去中心化多智能体协议，无需训练或中心规划器，在可扩展性和容错性上优于Star与Shared Context基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体通信协议多采用刚性结构，导致协调瓶颈，且随智能体数量增加性能退化。因此需要一种去中心化、可自适应扩展的通信机制。

Method: 提出Proxifield，一种轮次自适应、去中心化决策的多智能体协议。它依据智能体语义邻近度的演化构建稀疏通信图，在推理时使用四种路由信号建立连接：直接寻址、信息需求、计划对齐、信息互补；无需模型训练，也无需中心规划器。

Result: 在Drone Search and Rescue与HiddenBench两个领域评测：性能随模型规模（35B→397B）提升，最大规模下优于所有基线。随团队规模增大，相对Star的任务奖励优势从N=5时的5.4%扩大到N=25的53.0%、N=50的59.5%；Shared Context持续最差。在永久智能体失效下，Proxifield保留73.6%无故障奖励，优于Shared Context（58.3%）和Star（38.8%）。

Conclusion: 去中心化、语义自适应的路由机制可同时提升多智能体系统的可扩展性与容错性。

Abstract: As LLM capabilities have expanded, multi-agent communication has emerged as an increasingly active area of research. Prevailing protocols often adopt rigid structures that introduce coordination bottlenecks and can degrade as the number of agents increases. We introduce Proxifield, a round-adaptive multi-agent protocol with decentralized agent decision-making that constructs sparse communication graphs from the evolving semantic proximity of agents. Without model training or a centralized planner, Proxifield connects agents using four routing signals derived at inference time: direct address, information needs, plan alignment, and information complementarity. We compare Proxifield with two representative coordination baselines, a centralized Star protocol and a decentralized Shared Context protocol, across two domains: Drone Search and Rescue and the collective-reasoning benchmark HiddenBench. We first ablate base-model capability and find that, in both domains, the performance of Proxifield improves with model size (35B -> 397B parameter model) and Proxifield outperforms all baselines at the largest scale. As team size increases, Proxifield's task-reward advantage over Star widens from 5.4% at (N=5) to 53.0% at (N=25) and 59.5% at (N=50), while Shared Context consistently underperforms both protocols. Proxifield is also substantially more robust to permanent agent failure, retaining 73.6% of its no-failure task reward under the most severe condition, compared with 58.3% for Shared Context and 38.8% for Star. These results demonstrate that decentralized, semantically adaptive routing can improve the scalability and fault tolerance of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [68] [CityLearn v3: A Configurable Simulation and Evaluation Framework for Realistic Control Studies of Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21570)
*Tiago Fonseca,Luis Lino Ferreira,Armando Sousa,Ava Mohammadi,Zoltan Nagy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CityLearn v3，一个可配置仿真与评估框架，用于在参与成员变化、设备可用性波动、服务截止时间及数据质量不理想等条件下研究可再生能源社区（REC）控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 REC 控制器研究常简化成员参与变化、设备可用性、服务截止时间和数据质量等因素，导致较低成本或削峰结果可能掩盖服务未交付或功率请求不可行的问题。

Method: 在统一仿真环境中建模成员与资产变化、柔性负荷截止时间、需求响应请求、本地能量共享及数据/设备故障；以建筑与相位功率限值约束可控请求，并用声明的时间步保证功率—能量核算一致；记录控制器输入，区分请求动作与实际施加到仿真设备的动作；提供参考控制器、服务与约束感知的性能指标及轨迹导出。

Result: 软件检查与应用示例考察了服务交付、电气约束、结算及场景变化；合成的<strong>高频轨迹回放</strong>表明，聚合可能掩盖短时尖峰，而年度能耗不变。

Conclusion: 这些记录使聚合性能可与服务失败、动作削减及参与者层面结果一并解读，从而更全面、可追溯地评估 REC 控制器。

Abstract: Renewable energy communities (RECs) coordinate buildings, photovoltaic generation, batteries, electric vehicles and flexible loads. Controller studies often simplify changing participation, equipment availability, service deadlines and data quality, so lower cost or peak demand can conceal missed services or infeasible power requests. This paper presents CityLearn v3, a configurable simulation and evaluation framework for REC control studies under these conditions. It represents changing members and assets, flexible-load deadlines, demand-response requests, local energy sharing, and data or equipment failures within one simulation environment. Building and phase power limits constrain controllable requests, while a declared timestep preserves consistent power-to-energy accounting. The framework records controller inputs and distinguishes requested actions from those applied to the simulated equipment. Reference controllers, service- and constraint-aware performance indicators, and trajectory exports support comparisons within and across communities. Software checks and application examples examine service delivery, electrical constraints, settlement and changing scenarios; a synthetic high-frequency trace replay illustrates how aggregation can conceal short peaks without changing annual energy. Together, these records allow aggregate performance to be interpreted alongside service failures, action reductions and participant-level outcomes.

</details>


### [69] [Bayesian Belief Layer for Controllable Opinion Dynamics in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21997)
*Hafsa Akbar,Daniel Platnick,Marjan Alirezaie,Hossein Rahnama*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出贝叶斯编年史代理（BCA），通过显式信念层将LLM代理的“信念”与“表达”分离，用单一先验强度参数κ控制说服开放性，从而可按需生成共识、持续分歧、坚定少数影响三种经典舆论动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在社会模拟中隐式地修改观点，其说服开放性既无法明确设定也无法验证，集体结果还会继承模型的训练先验。作者希望引入一个最小的显式信念层，使观点动态可控、可验证、可审计。

Method: BCA将每个立场表示为概率，每听到一次发言就执行一次贝叶斯更新；用单一先验强度参数κ刻画“固执程度”，其建模灵感来自Friedkin-Johnsen（FJ）舆论动力学；随后扫描κ以按需产生三种典型舆论动力学机制，并检验κ在语言往返后是否仍可恢复。

Result: 持续分歧机制与FJ闭式不动点吻合，R²=0.93–0.99；预设κ在语言往返后仍可恢复，且在四个模型上均实现完美的秩序恢复；显式信念层还能揭示各模型系统性的立场偏差，而这些偏差在端到端模拟中会被静默吸收。

Conclusion: 显式信念层使LLM社会模拟更可控、可验证、可审计：κ可调节并可在语言往返后恢复，三种舆论动力学机制可按需生成，模型特有的立场偏差也可被识别。

Abstract: LLM agents in social simulation revise their opinions implicitly, in context: how open an agent is to persuasion can neither be specified nor verified, and collective outcomes inherit the model's training prior. We introduce Bayesian Chronicle Agents (BCA), a minimal belief layer separating \emph{what} an agent believes from \emph{how} it speaks. Each stance is a probability, updated by one Bayesian step per utterance heard. A single prior-strength parameter $κ$ encodes stubbornness, modeled after its role in Friedkin--Johnsen (FJ) opinion dynamics. We then sweep this parameter to yield three canonical regimes of opinion dynamics on demand (consensus, persistent disagreement, committed-minority influence), with persistent disagreement matching the FJ closed-form fixed points at $R^2\!=\!0.93$--$0.99$. We further show that prescribed $κ$ remains recoverable after the language round-trip, with perfect rank-order recovery across all four models. Explicit belief also makes simulation auditable: the layer surfaces systematic per-model stance biases that end-to-end simulation would silently absorb.

</details>

### [70] [Verify, Don't Trust: Agentic Model Development for Video Discovery Retrieval at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21257)
*Hao Fu,Baiting Zhu,Minglei Chen,Yinjie Huang,Shuai Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoPilot——一种人工把关的长时程在线自动研究方法，通过角色化智能体、版本化领域技能与类型适配器执行实验并留存可复用记录。在37天VDD检索系统优化行动中，修正了导致离线命中率虚降22个百分点的评估缺陷，修复后离线指标提升3.20个百分点，7天在线随机实验显示GSRR相对提升0.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体的自动研究循环可在分钟级、自包含程序上迭代，但真实在线自动研究涉及异步系统、小时级变体与周级行动，且易因空操作代码改动、数据窗口泄漏、评估语义漂移、两臂服务漏斗不一致等产生无效结论。既有手工实验未验证交互头的收益，而朴素自动研究尝试把22个百分点的离线命中率下降错误归因于该交互头，暴露了对实验有效性缺乏把关的问题。

Method: EvoPilot采用人工把关的长时程在线自动研究方法：每轮由角色专属智能体通过版本化领域技能与类型适配器执行；利用持久化记录保留实验与失败信息；通过确定性检查强制执行已记录的经验教训，并结合人工把关验证、回放与变异测试来区分有效与无效比较。

Result: 在VDD检索系统37天行动（覆盖7个方向、亿级视频小时级刷新索引）中，EvoPilot溯源发现离线命中率22个百分点下降实为输出深度3000与600的既有评估缺陷；修复后匹配比较显示离线提升3.20个百分点。回放与变异测试拒绝无效比较、接纳有效对照；持久状态恢复了被中断的一轮，工件复用节省约5 GPU小时。7天在线随机实验估计VDD片段GSRR相对提升0.66%。

Conclusion: EvoPilot表明，长时程在线自动研究需以人工把关、确定性检查与持久化状态保障实验结论的有效性；该方法不仅能纠正错误归因、避免无效对比，还可通过状态恢复与工件复用提升效率，并最终在真实在线指标上取得可测收益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can propose, implement, and evaluate model changes. Autoresearch loops demonstrate this capability through minutes-scale iterations on a self-contained program. Online autoresearch instead spans asynchronous systems, hours-long variants, and weeks-long campaigns that can influence a product. A completed run can still support an invalid conclusion when a code change is a no-op, data windows leak, evaluator semantics drift, or the two arms traverse different serving funnels. We present EvoPilot, a human-gated method for long-horizon online autoresearch. Role-specific agents execute each round through a versioned domain skill and typed adapter. Durable records preserve experiments and failures; deterministic checks enforce recorded lessons.
  We study a 37-day campaign for the retrieval system that powers Video Deep Dive (VDD), an online experience for discovering follow-on videos after a user opens a seed video. The campaign covered seven directions and used an hourly refreshed index of hundreds of millions of videos. Earlier manual experiments had not established a benefit from an interaction head. A primitive autoresearch attempt revisited the direction but incorrectly attributed an offline hit-rate decline of 22 percentage points to the head. We then introduced EvoPilot. Its human-gated verification traced the drop to a pre-existing evaluation defect that produced output depths of 3,000 and 600. After repair, a matched comparison measured an offline improvement of 3.20 percentage points. Post-study replay and mutation tests rejected invalid comparisons while admitting valid counterparts. Durable state recovered an interrupted round, and artifact reuse avoided approximately five GPU-hours. Separately, a seven-day randomized online evaluation estimated a 0.66% relative increase in the VDD slice of Good Search Result Rate for Retention (GSRR).

</details>
